### 清单及 astra 相应改动与作者规则的冲突（resolved_from_repo）

- 结论：1）否定式和元陈述（规则：除非必要，不写某性质“不保证”什么）：astra.txt:83“补充表 ST04……不能单独排除额外零模态”；:943 同类；:971“它不替代全部局部自由胞元的零空间条件”；:545“……成本未按等总时间比较”；:891“它尚未回答……总成本孰低”；:651“设计用途还须以共享参数梯度及最终性能检验”；:907“这些结果确定了当前证据范围”；:905“尚不能替代……”。清单第一部分各条“当前文字边界”所提倡的正是这类句子。2）只应说明我们为何采用本路线，不评论其他路线；不批评 PIML 组：清单第 3 项的“扩展的条件：只有准备声称最终设计性能受损时，才需要匹配的优化运行”。所谓“受限边界导致最终设计受损”的论断，实质是针对边界缩减的学习子结构（Huang、Guo 等），应永不提出。astra.txt:883“这说明单靠提高内部求解精度不能弥补固定边界表示的不足……分区加密、界面富集……仍可改变这一精度与规模的权衡”，既是笼统评价，又是逐项对照其他路线。astra.txt:907“与……Jiang, C. et al. (2026) 相比，NICE 仍存在取向差异”是与 PIML 文献逐项比较（R7_NOTES 将 Jiang et al. 2026 列入 PIML 论文）。3）不提重复测量：清单第 2 项要求“再选一个已有八胞元装配核对总时间”，第三部分 A 段并列旧证据包与当前计时（38.65/37.74/81.24/110.12 s 对 39.4/37.3/79.2/105.5 s）。任何重新计时都不得以重测的形式进入论文。4）过强用词：astra.txt:545 的“证明”。5）与 R1 已有决定冲突（不属于作者规则）：第 2 项“若学习只在……复用次数下节省时间”和第三部分 C 段的“回本次数”，会重新引入 R1 已删去的回本类成本说法（“cheaper for any number of queries”）。6）第 1 项的检验设计有技术问题（不属于规则）：K 对称，KR 与 RᵀK 是同一项检查；“接近零”没有判据，会把近机构的条件数问题当作零空间问题。
- 依据：astra.txt:83,545,651,883,891,905,907,943,971；P1_补实验与资料补核清单 第一部分第 1–3 项、第三部分 A、C；rewrite_plain/R7_NOTES.md（Working rules；PIML 论文列表）；review_r1/REVISION_PLAN_R1.md:4-15；S1_EN.md:54
- 建议：上列 astra 句子全部不采纳。清单中第 2 项（等精度总成本）和第 3 项的“设计受损”分支不予启动。第三部分 A、C 的资料补核可以做，但写入论文时只报告当前记录，不提旧值，也不提重测。

## 结构调整

Do not adopt astra's draft as a whole. Port selected changes into the source units (CN and EN together). Reasons:

(1) Astra worked from 7cd1595 without R7_NOTES. Its text reverses many decisions the author settled on 11-12 Oct:
- abstract wording, including the author's own fine-scale sentence and 'assumption no longer holds';
- intro ¶2 premises, the ¶3 numbers, the ¶4 ending on what cut cells require, the PCG in ¶5, the in-words guarantee in ¶6;
- the contribution list;
- the Section 2 outline sentence;
- the Section 3 requirement-to-section mapping;
- the homogenisation statement in the conclusions;
- 二阶小量 in Section 4 and the conclusions.

(2) The English is primary and must stay paragraph-parallel. Mirroring astra would delete the substantive content listed under dropped content from the submitted English, which the rules forbid. The EN abstract is already at 250 words and cannot take astra's longer abstract.

(3) Astra's register breaks the author's style rules:
- 它 appears 16 times in astra.txt against 6 in ours;
- '甚至可能' appears in intro ¶3;
- hedging meta-statements roughly double ('不能' 14 vs 7 in the main text; new '尚未', '仍需' ×4, '不预设');
- captions gain explanation;
- 6.2 attributes the speed-up partly to CPU/GPU differences and notes missing offline-cost records;
- 6.3 adds a '与 Jiang, C. et al. (2026) 相比' comparison with a PIML-group paper.

(4) The reorder (old 5.8 to 5.3, old 5.4 to Appendix J, figures renumbered) is a matter of taste with a real cost. It breaks the verification hierarchy that leads up to the lattice result. It leaves an incomplete roadmap and a few wrong pointers in astra's own text. It makes about 57 references in the CN/EN supplement stale, plus labels, FIGURES.md and the assembler order. Inside astra's main text and appendices, however, the numbering is consistent.

What to port, in priority order:
- (a) PCG/preconditioner description into 3.6, with the remark that only the interior-block factorisation of Eq. (2) is avoided (per-cell coarse factor and global K_PP factor remain); Algorithm 1 re-scoped.
- (b) Table 2 third row and the 591/≤153 column.
- (c) Table 3 column relabel, and fix our own '7 configurations' to 10 per ST09 (both languages).
- (d) Parameter domain from 6.3 to 5.1, keeping a pointer in 6.3.
- (e) Local corrections where astra is right:
  - intro ¶7: the compliance error is bounded by, not equal to, the weighted sum;
  - D.1 and 3.4: 'non-decreasing and not above the reference', and convergence only under strict contraction;
  - 2.2: ghost penalty only inside cells;
  - 5.1: the 60 geometries are independent only of checkpoint selection;
  - Table 4 note: NICE above base+correction on M2/H2/U2;
  - captions of old Figs. 13c/14;
  - 79-105 s should read 79-106 s (Table 5 gives 105.5 s);
  - remove '（命题 3 和命题 4）' from our intro ¶7, which breaks our own no-propositions-in-the-introduction rule.
- (f) Author decisions: whether the abstract and conclusions should state the independent-lattice result (0.015%, 0.09%), and whether to pair the 27% homogenisation error with NICE accuracy on the same (initial) design. Both are trade-offs within the 250-word budget.

Everything ported should be rewritten in our register and entered in U-files CN and EN together, then assembled and checked as usual.

### Old 5.8 (lattices made only of learned cells) moved to new 5.3, '独立胞元的完整装配验证', right after reference verification. Old 5.3-5.7 shift to 5.4-5.8, and new 5.5 becomes a one-paragraph summary. Astra's own 5.3 does not describe the PCG solve: the balanced two-level preconditioner text went to 3.6.

- 优点：Reader and referee meet the strongest evidence first: 16 cells never used in training or checkpoint selection, every cell learned, compliance error <=0.015% and shared-parameter gradient error <=0.09%. This answers the first question a CMAME/IJNME referee asks of a learned surrogate (generalisation, error accumulation) before any diagnostic material. Results-first ordering is common in application-oriented CMAME papers. A side effect is good: the PCG and preconditioner description now sits in the method section (3.6), so 5.9 and 5.10 cite 3.6 instead of a results section.
- 缺点：It breaks the bottom-up chain the theory follows: single-cell epsilon, then two-cell with one learned cell (w*epsilon, beta, Prop. 3 shown to be tight), then all-learned lattice. The lattice section explains its result with beta and Eqs. (15)/(16), which in astra's order come before the two-cell section that motivates them. To make the move work astra cut ours' motivation ('相邻胞元的误差进入同一个装配解…是否累积') and the comparison '点阵误差小于第 5.6 节两胞元装配中的最大误差'. 5.4-5.8 then read as a retrospective explanation of the headline number, whereas referees in this field expect a verification hierarchy: operator, component, assembly, application. Other knock-on effects: 5.4 forward-cites Table 4 (in 5.6); Table 3 row order no longer follows section order; astra's intro roadmap (line 35) leaves out 5.7 and 5.8 entirely. Renumbering cost: units U08-U10 (CN/EN), the assembler order, ~27 section/figure references in SUPPLEMENTARY_CN.md and ~30 in SUPPLEMENTARY_EN.md (astra did not touch the supplement), FIGURES.md, LaTeX labels and any reviewer-response mapping.
- 建议：Author decision; I lean towards keeping ours. Most of the benefit is available without renumbering: one sentence in the Section 5 opening stating the independent-lattice result, and the same in the abstract/conclusions if the 250-word EN budget allows. Port the PCG/preconditioner description to 3.6 either way (see Algorithm 1).

### Old 5.4 'network error distribution' moved to new Appendix J; the main text keeps one summary paragraph (new 5.5). The moved material covers the Jacobi spectrum, the spatial distribution near the cut, the Rayleigh-quotient identity epsilon=delta^2*kappa and the linear/quadratic split of the sensitivity error. Old Figures 6 and 7 become J.1 and J.2.

- 优点：Shorter main text: astra reports 80 to 73 rendered pages for the whole document. The spectral and spatial plots explain the mechanism; they are not evidence for the headline claims. New 5.6 (fixed-network correction) still carries the numbers that show the correction effect, and astra kept every number from old 5.4 in J.
- 缺点：The kappa/delta result ('about 1% interior displacement error gives 1.8-35% energy error because the error is stiff') is one of the paper's distinctive insights. Ours cites it in 6.1 and the conclusions; astra deleted both citations, so the insight leaves the body. The linear-versus-quadratic split of the sensitivity error is the body's only empirical support for Prop. 4 (the linear term takes over as the error goes to zero), and a referee checking the Section 4 to Section 5 link will look for it there. The summary paragraph gives only M1 numbers and asserts 'NICE 同时降低总误差及其局部集中程度' without a number. F.2, B.3 and 3.3 references had to be rewired; one of them is now wrong (3.3 cites 5.6 for the chi ratios).
- 建议：Keep ours. If length must come down: move only Figure 7 (spatial distribution) and its paragraph to an appendix, and keep the spectrum, the kappa/delta paragraph and the sensitivity split in Section 5. Any move is the author's decision.

### Figures renumbered: old 6 and 7 become J.1 and J.2; old 8-16 become 6-14. Captions were rewritten along the way: new Fig. 9 title neutralised, Fig. 11c clarified, Fig. 12 path versus same-design check, Fig. 2 and Fig. 3 captions extended.

- 优点：Follows consistently from the moves above. Inside main text plus appendices astra's numbering is coherent: Figures 1-14 are cited in order, J.1 and J.2 sit in Appendix J, and no stale 图 15/16 remains. Two caption clarifications are worth having. New 11c says the right panel is the absolute difference of displacement magnitudes, while 1.04% is the relative energy norm of the vector difference. New 12 separates 'two optimisation paths' from 'same design, exact check'.
- 缺点：Pure cost unless the 5.4 and 5.8 moves are adopted. The supplement is not updated, for example '第 5.5 节修正实验', '图 9 中的相邻胞元', '第 5.4 节中基础网络', '第 5.5 节，图 8a', 'S04: 遵循图 11 中的关系', '图 15'. FIGURES.md, LaTeX labels, figure file order and review responses would all change. Astra also added explanation to captions, against the caption rule: Fig. 2 '网络内部的多层传播见图 3，它与图中按刚度残差执行的两重网格修正分开' (which also uses 它), and Fig. 3 '这些操作构成网络初值，不是第 3.3–3.4 节的刚度残差修正'. The Fig. 5 caption now lists its panels in the order (a), (c), (b).
- 建议：Follow the decision on the 5.4 and 5.8 moves. If ours is kept, keep Figures 1-16. Port the clarifications for old Figs. 13c and 14 separately, trimmed to 'what is shown and how to read it'. Reject the additions to the Fig. 2 and Fig. 3 captions.

### From old 6.3: the parameter domain (corner parameters in [0.175, 0.699], span and gradient norm <=0.47, one plane cut with normal (cos theta, sin theta, 0) up to cube symmetry, n=32/E=1/nu=0.3/gamma=1e-4, grid hierarchy 65/33/17/9) moved to the first paragraph of 5.1.

- 优点：Scope of validity is stated before any result, which referees appreciate. 5.10's bounds and limits can point back to it ('第 5.1 节的训练参数域') instead of forward to the Discussion, and the duplication between 5.10 and 6.3 in ours disappears.
- 缺点：Minor. 6.3 must keep a pointer; astra's '当前精度限于第 5.1 节所定义的胞族与离散范围' does that. In the shuffle astra lost '主要在恒等取向下评估' and the definition of the missing equivariance.
- 建议：Adopt: port into U07 (CN and EN). Keep in 6.3 a one-line pointer plus our sentence that NICE was evaluated mainly in the identity orientation.

### From old 6.3: the solver-comparison scope and the scale bottleneck moved to 6.2. These are AMG/BoomerAMG/BDDC only on the two eight-cell lattices with no FETI-DP or tuned multigrid; cut-plane DOFs are 37-50% of free retained DOFs; the direct factorisation of K_PP limits size on one GPU.

- 优点：Everything about cost sits together in 6.2, and 6.3 becomes a section on accuracy scope.
- 缺点：Astra's new 6.2 replaces our account of why NICE is faster. Ours: exact routes solve a fine-scale problem per cell or per lattice, NICE uses one network pass plus a fixed correction and solves only on the retained DOFs. Astra: '收益既包括局部凝聚作用，也包括几何准备、装配及 CPU/GPU 实现的差异'. That undercuts the paper's own result and breaks the rule 'explain why our route is taken'. It also adds a sentence that exposes missing records ('离线成本的摊销还需完整的生成与训练用时记录'), which does not belong in a paper. It drops 'the assembled solve dominates NICE time (ST12d)', the fact that motivates reducing the cut-plane DOFs. The limitations end up split between 6.2 and 6.3.
- 建议：Author decision. Acceptable only if our sentences are moved into 6.2 unchanged (with our explanation of the speed-up). Reject astra's new 6.2 wording.

### From old 6.3: the discrete-switching and full-derivative details moved to the end of Appendix H.2. These cover: s_c ignores dF/dtau; central differences cannot decide between C^,c and s_c because 21-48 of 49 rebuilds switch a discrete choice; shared-parameter gradient errors 0.02-0.13% / 0.22-0.35% (ST14); no line search.

- 优点：Removes technical detail from the Discussion. 4.2 already states the choice of s_c, and H.2 is where Eq. (18) and s_c are analysed.
- 缺点：These are limitations: s_c is not the derivative of the approximate compliance, and finite differences cannot arbitrate. Moving a limitation to an appendix can look to a referee like hiding it. Astra also drops the reason for having no line search ('线搜索会将近似柔度与并非其导数的 s_c 相比较'), the statement that 'the optimisations are local and the bounds keep the design in the training domain', and, from 4.2, the bound that a discrete switch adds a jump no larger than beta*C. The main text loses the ST14 gradient numbers for the four-cell layers.
- 建议：Keep ours: a condensed 6.3 sentence that includes the line-search reason. The 49-rebuild statistic may go to H.2 if the author wants a shorter 6.3.

### Introduction contributions cut from four to three. Ours (ours.txt lines 29-35; U00_CN.md and U00_EN.md lines 31-34) has four: (1) retain all face and cut-plane-element DOFs, with the 5.7 ablation pointer; (2) a network for retained sets that change with the cut, with the energy properties carrying over; (3) a fixed two-grid correction included in training, base network 6.89% to 0.0965%; (4) the error routes into compliance and sensitivity, both checked, designs verified against exact condensation and compared with homogenisation. Astra has three: (1) a network for full retained sets (merging our 1 and 2); (2) learned initial field plus fixed correction plus full transpose as an assemblable operator, with structural properties and the non-increase condition; (3) error relations plus validation by independent assembly, ablation, exact-condensation design check and online cost.

- 优点：Shorter. Puts the independent-assembly validation into the list and removes repeated numbers.
- 缺点：The author settled items (1)-(3) on 12 Oct (R7_NOTES): retained DOFs as their own item, the network, and the correction with 6.89% to 0.0965%. R7 also says 'the classical property is not claimed as new'; astra's (2) '由离散能量建立结构性质' reads as claiming it. Retaining the DOFs stops being a contribution of its own. The reason supports and loads can act directly, and 'one network for different cuts without retraining', leave the list. Matching the EN would delete content from the submitted text, which the rules forbid.
- 建议：Keep ours. Optionally add to our item (4) a few words on the lattices of independent cells.

### Section 3.6 rewritten and 'Algorithm 1' re-scoped. Algorithm 1 is not new: ours already has '算法 1. 修正后凝聚刚度矩阵的作用' (ours.txt l.211; U05_EN 'Algorithm 1'). Astra splits 3.6 into three parts: per-design preparation, one local action, and the global PCG with the balanced two-level preconditioner (moved from old 5.8). It adds that only the interior-block factorisation of Eq. (2) is avoided, while the per-cell coarse factor and the global K_PP factorisation remain. Algorithm 1 is re-titled '几何准备完成后' and its step 3 now covers the global iteration, recovery and design update.

- 优点：The method section describes the whole solver, and 5.9 route (c) and 5.10 can cite it. The factorisation clarification heads off an obvious referee objection to 'avoids factorisation', since V^T A V and K_PP are factorised. The algorithm box now covers the full loop.
- 缺点：Register: '这里避免的是', '这个粗空间', several 它. Step 3 mixes the solver with the design loop, which is acceptable under the new title.
- 建议：Adopt the content: port into U05 (CN and EN) in our register, and move the PCG paragraph out of U10.

### Table 2: a third row 'Base network + correction' (same checkpoint, no retraining, evaluation correction 8/Q1(17)/8), and the training-set column changed to '候选 591；实际至多 153' and '305 个训练几何'. Astra's checklist states the new variant reuses the base checkpoint.

- 优点：The third variant gets its own row. Separating the 591 candidates from the <=153 geometries actually used removes a likely referee misreading of '591 training geometries'. The numbers match ours' text (591 contains 304 of the 305; at most 153 used).
- 缺点：None of substance. The paragraph that introduces the third variant becomes partly redundant, and the EN table must match.
- 建议：Adopt (CN and EN).

### Table 3: column '胞元（切割）' became '每构型胞元数（切割）' with entries such as '2（0或1）' and '2（1）'. The configuration counts moved into the load column ('14个构型；各六个面载荷'), and the section numbers follow the new order (5.7, 5.3).

- 优点：The cell column now holds one kind of quantity, and the configuration count sits with the loads.
- 缺点：The section column depends on the reorder. Under astra the rows are no longer in section order: 5.7 rows come before 5.3 rows. Both drafts say '7 个构型' for cut-surface loads, while Supplementary Table ST09 lists 10 NICE configurations (M1, H1, M2, H3, L1, each x and y). The number 7 matches the uncorrected-continuation variant ('其七种构型'). Astra left a [TBD] instead of checking ST09.
- 建议：Adopt the column relabel and keep our section numbers. Separately, correct 7 to 10 in CN and EN (U07) once the ST09 source has been checked. The maxima <=0.10 / <=0.16 already match ST09's NICE rows (0.101 and 0.161).

### 交叉引用问题

- Overall: all 命题 1-4 and 命题 4(b), equation (1)-(18) and (A.1)-(H.7), 表 1-6 and 图 1-14/J.1-J.2 references in astra.txt resolve to existing objects, and no stale '第 5.4/5.5 节' remains. The problems below are wrong targets, incomplete roadmaps or targets outside astra's file.
- Introduction roadmap (astra l.35): '第 5 节依次报告参考模型核验、独立装配、单胞精度与机制对照、计算成本和厚度设计' omits 5.7 (two-cell assemblies) and 5.8 (boundary ablation). The roadmap does not match astra's own numbering.
- Section 3.3 (l.177): '附录 J 与第 5.6 节用这些比例分析网络和修正分别处理的误差分量'. New 5.6 does not use the chi_l ratios; the 24.5%/4.87% figures are in 5.5 and App. J. Should read 第 5.5 节 (or App. J only).
- Section 5.8 ablation (l.651): '设计用途还须以共享参数梯度及最终性能检验，见第 5.3 和 5.10 节'. Neither section tests the boundary-restricted representation, so the pointer implies a check that does not exist; astra's own checklist item 3 lists it as missing.
- Section 5.10 (l.759): '主要评估取向 0<theta<pi/4（第 5.1 和 6.3 节）'. Astra's 6.3 no longer says NICE was evaluated mainly in the identity orientation (only the 4%/12% gap remains), so the 6.3 target is stale.
- Abstract (l.5) cites '表 5'. A table reference in an abstract is not self-contained, and the EN abstract cannot carry it.
- Appendix B.1 (l.957) still uses '粗因子平移量 xi（附录 F.1）', but astra's F.1 no longer defines the term '粗因子平移' (ours F.1: '所选平移值称为粗因子平移').
- Table 3 row order (5.7, 5.7, 5.7, 5.3, 5.3, 5.10, ...) does not follow astra's section order.
- Table 3, cut-surface row: '2（1）… 7个构型'. Inconsistent with Supplementary Table ST09, which lists 10 NICE configurations (M1, H1, M2, H3, L1 × x, y); our draft has the same error.
- Supplement not renumbered: astra claims all references were synchronised, but its file holds only main text and appendices. Under astra's numbering these SUPPLEMENTARY_CN.md references become stale: '第 5.5 节修正实验' (should be 5.6), '图 9 中的相邻胞元' (图 7), '第 5.4 节中基础网络' (附录 J), '第 5.5 节，图 8a' (5.6, 图 6a), '第 5.6 节' ×4 (5.7), '第 5.8 节' ×9 (5.3), '第 5.8–5.10 节' (5.3, 5.9, 5.10), '图 11' ×2 (图 9), '图 15' (图 13), '图 6' (图 J.1). SUPPLEMENTARY_EN.md has about 30 more.
- Fig. 5 caption (l.533) describes the panels in the order (a), (c), (b), with '两图' referring to (a) and (c).
- Appendix J (l.1389) opens with '本节' although it is an appendix; minor.

### 被删去的内容

- [absent] Abstract: the author's own fine-scale sentence ('细尺度有限元模型须离散全部胞元，每次设计迭代都需重新组装和求解大规模方程组'), the homogenisation-failure sentence and the static-condensation sentence. The periodic-BC failure statement ('周期性边界条件假设在不满足尺度分离或胞元被切割时不再成立') is gone from the whole paper, including the intro and the conclusions that R7 aligned with the abstract; '周期性' occurs 0 times in astra.txt.
- [changed, author-settled] Abstract: plate accuracy changed from the final design (0.03%/0.3%) to the initial design (0.011%/0.029%). New conditional and hedging text ('在所述离散模型与再现条件下', '误差大小仍取决于恢复精度', 'CPU/GPU 部署与表 5 所列工作量下'), which R7 rejected for the abstract.
- [absent] Intro ¶2: the two premises of homogenisation (scale separation; complete, periodic cells) and how each fails; replaced by '可能使这一近似失准'.
- [absent] Intro ¶3: interior DOFs about 6× the retained ones (median, Fig. 1b), stated nowhere else. The statement 16-20 min exact condensation vs 14-17 min direct solve is replaced by '甚至可能超过整体直接求解' (甚至 is on the avoid list); the numbers survive only in Table 5. The 0.60-9.4 GiB factors survive in 5.9.
- [absent] Intro ¶4: the boundary-reduction examples (linear or cubic Bézier interpolation, one shape function per boundary DOF). Also the author-settled closing on what cut cells require: supports and loads on the cut surface act on all nodes of the cut-plane elements, so all of them are retained and the set changes with geometry. It is replaced by '本文因此选择保留…', which justifies our route by the other route's limitation.
- [absent from intro] ¶5: PCG for the assembled system (R7 item), and 'smoothing reduces high-frequency, coarse correction low-frequency components' ('低频' occurs 0 times in astra). ¶6: the in-words explanation of the correction guarantee that the author asked for on 12 Oct; it survives only symbolically in 3.3/3.4. ¶7: '能量误差对该项只能给出平方根量级的界'.
- [absent] 'In the same correction the graph-harmonic field leaves 5.4-265× the error of NICE': gone from intro, Section 5 opening, 5.3 and conclusions (only the raw data in Table 4 remain).
- [absent] Contributions: see the contributions move. Lost from the list are the 6.89% to 0.0965% figure (it survives in 5.4), 'one network for different cuts without retraining' and the 5.7 boundary-error pointer.
- [absent] Section 1.1: the energy property shared by condensation and component reduction (upper bound, second-order error, minimum potential energy; '最小势能原理' occurs 0 times in astra). Also 'network takes the cut-dependent discrete geometry as input' and 'energy properties of static condensation hold for NICE' from the closing paragraph. A positioning comparison was added to ¶1 ('以共享网络代替逐几何构造内部基'), against R7's rule that only the closing paragraph compares.
- [absent] Section 2: the one-sentence opening outline (author request, 12 Oct); 2.2 '刚度矩阵趋于病态' for small cuts; 2.4 shared DOFs 'identified by their position on the background grid'.
- [absent] Section 3 opening: the mapping of the three requirements to 3.1 / Section 4 / 3.2-3.4 (author decision on 12 Oct), and '修正步数还可在部署时增加而无需重新训练'.
- [absent from body] 3.1: the full transpose makes the stiffness error second order (only Eq. (B.6) remains). 3.2: the cost of one network pass 'does not depend on the number of retained nodes'. 3.3: the motivation that Eq. (7) and Eq. (14) both call for reducing the interior force imbalance at fixed retained displacements.
- [absent] 4.2: at a discrete switch the approximate compliance adds a jump no larger than beta*C (0<=C−Ĉ<=βC, S4.3).
- [absent] Old 5.8: the motivation (neighbour errors enter one assembled solution: do they accumulate?) and 'the lattice error is below the largest two-cell error, because of Eq. (15)'.
- [absent] Old 5.7: the conclusion 'restricted interfaces satisfy compliance at low degree but not local sensitivity under neighbour-face loads'.
- [absent] 5.9: the opening result and 'two reasons' paragraph (assembly solved only on retained DOFs; one network pass plus fixed correction, no interior factorisation); AMG = 'eight and seven times NICE'; setup and assembly alone '5.3 and 4.5 times NICE'; the pointer to the 32-thread and 1-thread timings (ST12b).
- [absent] 5.10: the summary 'homogenisation underestimates the cut layer's compliance by about a quarter on both macro meshes, and its design is less stiff on the cut geometry'. 5.6: the identification of the six ST07 cells ('表 4 中的胞元和 U1').
- [absent] 6.1: 'the local sensitivity error from restricting cell faces exceeds the error of NICE's interior approximation (5.6, 5.7)'; 'homogenisation needs an extra constraint on the cut plane to represent the support'; 'the assembled solve dominates NICE analysis time (ST12d)'; 'the same network, unchanged, served the 80 validation geometries, the 16 lattice cells and the plate's cut cells'.
- [absent] 6.2: why NICE is faster (exact routes solve fine-scale problems per cell or per lattice, by direct or iterative methods); 'fewer smoothing steps lower the cost of applying S'. Replaced by an attribution to CPU/GPU differences and a remark about missing offline-cost records.
- [absent] 6.3: gyroid errors 'reduced six- to tenfold' by continued training; cells with several cuts or curved boundaries; training on several families and testing on a held-out one as future work; 'NICE was evaluated mainly in the identity orientation' and the definition of the missing equivariance; the enumeration of validated assemblies (14+18 two-cell configurations, two lattices, two four-cell layers) and of exactly verified designs (3 in case A, 5 plate designs, the 24- and 51-cell initial designs); the reason for no line search; 'the bounds keep designs in the parameter domain; the optimisations are local'.
- [absent] Conclusions: ST07 'more smoothing steps reduced the error in every case'; 5.4-265×; 6.89% to 0.0965%; 1% displacement error gives 1.8-35% energy error; 'the relations hold for any approximate recovery operator'; two-cell maxima 0.27%/1.49%; direct solve 868-1,042 s; homogenisation underestimates compliance by 27%, with the periodic-BC failure sentence (R7 decision); scale-study accuracy <0.014% / <0.05%; the outlook of folding the coarse correction into the network as a coarse level; 'error relations and correction carry over unchanged to new families' (weakened).
- [absent] Appendices: F.1 'for cut cells near the lower thickness bound this rule keeps the forward and transpose coarse solves consistent' and the definition of '粗因子平移' (B.1 still uses the term); D 'for walls about one element thick'. [changed] 2.3/A.1: ST04 no longer 'supports' the null-space condition under numerical integration. This weakens a claim; whether to weaken it, or to run astra's suggested null-space check, is an author decision.
